# Removal stage 2 — test statements removed, ported or restored

One line per change to a test script made while removing the Inbox, Gmail, Krisp, Assist and
the email send (see docs/removal-log.md, "Stage 2"). Format:

  <file>:<first line>-<last line> [<why>] <first 140 characters of the statement>

Line numbers are the file's at the time of that pass, not today's. Reasons:

  import / import-member   an import of a deleted module, or one name from it
  dead-path <path>         a string naming a deleted source file (a source-pin read)
  TS####                   the statement no longer compiled once the above were gone
  prop                     one property removed from an object literal
  runtime FAIL <message>   the assertion failed because it pinned removed code or text
  manual: ...              done by hand — a block or suite deleted, an assertion updated to
                           the new source or copy, a test ported to a kept example, or the
                           async suite chain restored (stage 1 had cut it)
